To address these limitations, we propose CPGNet, an asymmetric RGB-polarization framework that introduces a conditional polarization guidance mechanism to explicitly regulate RGB feature learning for camouflaged object detection. Specifically, we design a lightweight polarization interaction module that jointly models these complementary cues and generates reliable polarization guidance in a unified manner. Unlike conventional feature fusion strategies, the proposed conditional guidance mechanism dynamically modulates RGB features using polarization priors, enabling the network to focus on subtle discrepancies between camouflaged objects and their backgrounds. Furthermore, we introduce a polarization edge-guided frequency refinement strategy that enhances high-frequency components under polarization constraints, effectively breaking camouflage patterns. Finally, we develop an iterative feedback decoder to perform coarse-to-fine feature calibration and progressively refine camouflage prediction. Extensive experiments on polarization datasets across multiple tasks, along with evaluations on non-polarization datasets, demonstrate that CPGNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [114] [Benchmarking PhD-Level Coding in 3D Geometric Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30038)
*Wenyi Li,Renkai Luo,Yue Yu,Huan-ang Gao,Mingju Gao,Li Yuan,Chaoyou Fu,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCodeBench是一个评估3D几何视觉编程能力的博士级基准测试，最佳模型GPT-5仅达到36.6%通过率，显示当前AI在复杂3D科学编码方面仍有巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助编码虽然改变了软件实践和研究工作流程，但在复杂3D几何视觉编码方面仍不可靠。如果模型能够可靠地编写此类代码，将极大改变社区的研究方式。需要建立基准来衡量这一目标的进展。

Method: 从近期会议代表性论文中收集函数实现任务，先让工具从官方仓库提出候选函数，然后人工筛选选择核心3D几何组件。为每个目标生成多样化的边缘情况单元测试，实现全自动、可重复的评分。将任务组织为两级层次：通用3D能力（几何变换和力学/光学公式）和研究能力（新算法实现和几何逻辑路由）。

Result: 评估了8个代表性开源和闭源模型，最佳模型GPT-5仅达到36.6%通过率。研究导向任务明显更难。上下文消融实验显示"更多论文文本"并不总是更好：在方法部分截断的输入在统计上优于完整论文输入，突显了长上下文科学理解方面的未解决挑战。

Conclusion: GeoCodeBench为从通用编码向可信赖的3D几何视觉编码的进步提供了一个严格的测试平台，揭示了当前能力与可靠3D科学编码之间的巨大差距。

Abstract: AI-assisted coding has rapidly reshaped software practice and research workflows, yet today's models still struggle to produce correct code for complex 3D geometric vision. If models could reliably write such code, the research of our community would change substantially. To measure progress toward that goal, we introduce GeoCodeBench, a PhD-level benchmark that evaluates coding for 3D vision. Each problem is a fill-in-the-function implementation task curated from representative papers at recent venues: we first let a tool propose candidate functions from official repositories, then perform careful human screening to select core 3D geometric components. For every target, we generate diverse, edge-case unit tests, enabling fully automatic, reproducible scoring. We evaluate eight representative open- and closed-source models to reflect the current ecosystem. The best model, GPT-5, attains only 36.6% pass rate, revealing a large gap between current capabilities and dependable 3D scientific coding. GeoCodeBench organizes tasks into a two-level hierarchy: General 3D capability (geometric transformations and mechanics/optics formulation) and Research capability (novel algorithm implementation and geometric logic routing). Scores are positively correlated across these axes, but research-oriented tasks are markedly harder. Context ablations further show that "more paper text" is not always better: cutting off at the Method section statistically outperforms full-paper inputs, highlighting unresolved challenges in long-context scientific comprehension. Together, these findings position GeoCodeBench as a rigorous testbed for advancing from generic coding to trustworthy 3D geometric vision coding.

</details>


### [115] [Video Models Reason Early: Exploiting Plan Commitment for Maze Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30043)
*Kaleb Newman,Tyler Zhu,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频扩散模型在早期去噪步骤中就确定了运动规划，路径长度（而非障碍密度）是迷宫难度的主要预测因素，通过链式生成和早期规划选择可显著提升长距离推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型展现出解决迷宫和谜题等推理能力，但其内部推理机制尚不清楚。本文旨在理解视频模型在生成过程中的内部规划动态，以2D迷宫解决作为受控测试平台。

Method: 使用2D迷宫解决作为测试平台，分析视频扩散模型的内部规划动态。提出ChEaP方法：通过早期规划选择有前景的种子，并将它们链式连接以处理复杂迷宫。

Result: 发现两个关键现象：1) 早期规划承诺：模型在前几个去噪步骤中就确定了高层运动规划；2) 路径长度（而非障碍密度）是迷宫难度的主要预测因素，存在12步的失败阈值。ChEaP方法将长距离迷宫准确率从7%提升到67%，在Frozen Lake和VR-Bench等任务上整体提升2.5倍。

Conclusion: 当前视频模型具有比先前认识更深的推理能力，通过更好的推理时缩放可以更可靠地激发这些能力。早期规划承诺现象和路径长度限制的发现为理解和改进视频模型的推理能力提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Video diffusion models exhibit emergent reasoning capabilities like solving mazes and puzzles, yet little is understood about how they reason during generation. We take a first step towards understanding this and study the internal planning dynamics of video models using 2D maze solving as a controlled testbed. Our investigations reveal two findings. Our first finding is early plan commitment: video diffusion models commit to a high-level motion plan within the first few denoising steps, after which further denoising alters visual details but not the underlying trajectory. Our second finding is that path length, not obstacle density, is the dominant predictor of maze difficulty, with a sharp failure threshold at 12 steps. This means video models can only reason over long mazes by chaining together multiple sequential generations. To demonstrate the practical benefits of our findings, we introduce Chaining with Early Planning, or ChEaP, which only spends compute on seeds with promising early plans and chains them together to tackle complex mazes. This improves accuracy from 7% to 67% on long-horizon mazes and by 2.5x overall on hard tasks in Frozen Lake and VR-Bench across Wan2.2-14B and HunyuanVideo-1.5. Our analysis reveals that current video models possess deeper reasoning capabilities than previously recognized, which can be elicited more reliably with better inference-time scaling.

</details>


### [116] [OmniRoam: World Wandering via Long-Horizon Panoramic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30045)
*Yuheng Liu,Xin Lin,Xinke Li,Baihan Yang,Chen Wang,Kalyan Sunkavalli,Yannick Hold-Geoffroy,Hao Tan,Kai Zhang,Xiaohui Xie,Zifan Shi,Yiwei Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniRoam是一个可控的全景视频生成框架，通过预览和精炼两阶段生成长期、高分辨率、全局一致的场景漫游视频，解决了现有视角视频模型在完整性和一致性方面的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数方法依赖于视角视频模型，只能合成场景的有限观察，导致完整性和全局一致性问题。需要一种能够生成长期、高保真场景漫游视频的方法。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：1) 预览阶段：使用轨迹控制的视频生成模型从输入图像/视频创建场景概览；2) 精炼阶段：将视频在时间上扩展、空间上上采样，生成长期高分辨率视频。使用合成和真实世界捕获的全景视频数据集进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，OmniRoam在视觉质量、可控性和长期场景一致性方面均优于现有最先进方法，定性和定量评估都表现出色。还展示了实时视频生成和3D重建等扩展应用。

Conclusion: OmniRoam通过利用全景表示的丰富场景覆盖和内在时空一致性，实现了高质量、可控的长期场景漫游视频生成，为场景建模提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Modeling scenes using video generation models has garnered growing research interest in recent years. However, most existing approaches rely on perspective video models that synthesize only limited observations of a scene, leading to issues of completeness and global consistency. We propose OmniRoam, a controllable panoramic video generation framework that exploits the rich per-frame scene coverage and inherent long-term spatial and temporal consistency of panoramic representation, enabling long-horizon scene wandering. Our framework begins with a preview stage, where a trajectory-controlled video generation model creates a quick overview of the scene from a given input image or video. Then, in the refine stage, this video is temporally extended and spatially upsampled to produce long-range, high-resolution videos, thus enabling high-fidelity world wandering. To train our model, we introduce two panoramic video datasets that incorporate both synthetic and real-world captured videos. Experiments show that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of visual quality, controllability, and long-term scene consistency, both qualitatively and quantitatively. We further showcase several extensions of this framework, including real-time video generation and 3D reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/yuhengliu02/OmniRoam.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [CREST: Constraint-Release Execution for Multi-Robot Warehouse Shelf Rearrangement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28803)
*Jiaqi Tan,Yudong Luo,Sophia Huang,Yifan Yang,Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CREST框架通过在执行过程中主动释放轨迹约束，解决了DD-MAPD问题中传统方法导致的代理闲置和不必要货架切换问题，显著提升了执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPF-DECOMP框架虽然高效，但强制执行严格的轨迹依赖关系，导致代理闲置和不必要的货架切换，降低了执行质量。

Method: 提出CREST执行框架，在执行过程中主动释放轨迹约束，实现更连续的货架搬运，减少代理闲置和货架切换。

Result: 在多样化仓库布局实验中，CREST相比MAPF-DECOMP将代理移动距离、总完成时间和货架切换分别减少了40.5%、33.3%和44.4%，在提升/放置开销下效果更显著。

Conclusion: 执行感知的约束释放对于可扩展的仓库重排至关重要，CREST框架通过主动释放约束显著提升了多机器人货架重排的执行效率。

Abstract: Double-Deck Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (DD-MAPD) models the multi-robot shelf rearrangement problem in automated warehouses. MAPF-DECOMP is a recent framework that first computes collision-free shelf trajectories with a MAPF solver and then assigns agents to execute them. While efficient, it enforces strict trajectory dependencies, often leading to poor execution quality due to idle agents and unnecessary shelf switching. We introduce CREST, a new execution framework that achieves more continuous shelf carrying by proactively releasing trajectory constraints during execution. Experiments on diverse warehouse layouts show that CREST consistently outperforms MAPF-DECOMP, reducing metrics related to agent travel, makespan, and shelf switching by up to 40.5\%, 33.3\%, and 44.4\%, respectively, with even greater benefits under lift/place overhead. These results underscore the importance of execution-aware constraint release for scalable warehouse rearrangement. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ChristinaTan0704/CREST.

</details>


### [118] [A Classification of Heterogeneity in Uncrewed Vehicle Swarms and the Effects of Its Inclusion on Overall Swarm Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28831)
*Abhishek Joshi,Abhishek Phadke,Tianxing Chu,F. Antonio Medrano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个系统框架，用于对异构无人载具集群进行分类，基于智能体性质、硬件结构和操作空间三个主要因素。分析表明异构集群通过利用多样化能力、动态适应角色和整合多维传感器数据，具有更强的韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无人载具集群中结合不同类型的智能体已成为增强任务韧性和操作能力的方法。需要系统框架来分类不同类型的集群，理解异构性如何提升性能，并解决实施中的挑战。

Method: 基于三个主要因素的系统分类框架：1) 智能体性质（行为和功能），2) 硬件结构（物理配置和感知能力），3) 操作空间（操作领域）。通过文献综述分析异构集群的性能优势。

Result: 异构集群显著提升集群性能，更具韧性，能够利用多样化能力、动态适应角色和整合多维传感器数据。战略异构性改善任务执行效果。

Conclusion: 异构集群技术正接近高价值应用的准备状态。研究提供了单一分类法和基于证据的观察，用于设计平衡复杂性和增强能力的任务就绪异构集群，需要考虑模拟到现实转移、标准化评估指标和可互操作控制架构。

Abstract: Combining different types of agents in uncrewed vehicle (UV) swarms has emerged as an approach to enhance mission resilience and operational capabilities across a wide range of applications. This study offers a systematic framework for grouping different types of swarms based on three main factors: agent nature (behavior and function), hardware structure (physical configuration and sensing capabilities), and operational space (domain of operation). A literature review indicates that strategic heterogeneity significantly improves swarm performance. Operational challenges, including communication architecture constraints, energy-aware coordination strategies, and control system integration, are also discussed. The analysis shows that heterogeneous swarms are more resilient because they can leverage diverse capabilities, adapt roles on the fly, and integrate data from multidimensional sensor feeds. Some important factors to consider when implementing are sim-to-real-world transfer for learned policies, standardized evaluation metrics, and control architectures that can work together. Learning-based coordination, GPS (Global Positioning System)-denied multi-robot SLAM (Simultaneous Localization and Mapping), and domain-specific commercial deployments collectively demonstrate that heterogeneous swarm technology is moving closer to readiness for high-value applications. This study offers a single taxonomy and evidence-based observations on methods for designing mission-ready heterogeneous swarms that balance complexity and increased capability.

</details>


### [119] [A Semantic Observer Layer for Autonomous Vehicles: Pre-Deployment Feasibility Study of VLMs for Low-Latency Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28888)
*Kunal Runwal,Swaraj Gajare,Daniel Adejumo,Omkar Ankalkope,Siddhant Baroth,Aliasghar Arab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语义观察层架构，使用量化视觉语言模型以1-2Hz频率监控自动驾驶中的语义异常，实现约500ms推理速度，满足安全监控需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义异常（像素级检测器无法推理的上下文相关危险）对自动驾驶构成关键安全风险，需要专门监控机制。

Method: 使用Nvidia Cosmos-Reason1-7B模型，采用NVFP4量化和FlashAttention2优化，构建语义观察层并行于主AV控制循环运行。

Result: 实现约500ms推理时间，比未优化的FP16基线快约50倍；识别出NF4量化导致的召回率崩溃（10.6%）作为硬部署约束。

Conclusion: 语义观察架构在具身AI自动驾驶平台上具有预部署可行性，通过性能指标到安全目标的映射分析建立了安全基础。

Abstract: Semantic anomalies-context-dependent hazards that pixel-level detectors cannot reason about-pose a critical safety risk in autonomous driving. We propose a \emph{semantic observer layer}: a quantized vision-language model (VLM) running at 1--2\,Hz alongside the primary AV control loop, monitoring for semantic edge cases, and triggering fail-safe handoffs when detected. Using Nvidia Cosmos-Reason1-7B with NVFP4 quantization and FlashAttention2, we achieve ~500 ms inference a ~50x speedup over the unoptimized FP16 baseline (no quantization, standard PyTorch attention) on the same hardware--satisfying the observer timing budget. We benchmark accuracy, latency, and quantization behavior in static and video conditions, identify NF4 recall collapse (10.6%) as a hard deployment constraint, and a hazard analysis mapping performance metrics to safety goals. The results establish a pre-deployment feasibility case for the semantic observer architecture on embodied-AI AV platforms.

</details>


### [120] [Bootstrap Perception Under Hardware Depth Failure for Indoor Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28890)
*Nishant Pushparaju,Vivek Mattam,Aliasghar Arab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自举感知系统，用于硬件深度传感器失效时的室内机器人导航，通过传感器自身有效像素校准学习式单目深度，在需要时填补深度信息缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内导航中，ToF相机在反射表面会丢失大量深度像素（可达78%），而2D LiDAR无法感知扫描平面以上的障碍物。需要解决硬件深度传感器失效时的感知问题。

Method: 利用传感器失效的自参照特性：有效像素校准学习式单目深度到度量尺度，形成故障感知层次结构：LiDAR作为几何锚点，硬件深度在有效时保留，学习深度仅在需要时填补空缺。

Result: 选择性融合在走廊和动态行人评估中，比单独使用LiDAR增加55-110%的障碍物覆盖率。紧凑蒸馏学生模型在Jetson Orin Nano上达到218FPS，在闭环模拟中实现9/10导航成功率且零碰撞，匹配地面真实深度基线但成本更低。

Conclusion: 该系统通过自校准学习深度填补硬件传感器失效的空缺，实现了故障感知的层次化融合，在保持低成本的同时提高了导航可靠性和障碍物覆盖率。

Abstract: We present a bootstrap perception system for indoor robot navigation under hardware depth failure. In our corridor data, the time-of-flight camera loses up to 78% of its depth pixels on reflective surfaces, yet a 2D LiDAR alone cannot sense obstacles above its scan plane. Our system exploits a self-referential property of this failure: the sensor's surviving valid pixels calibrate learned monocular depth to metric scale, so the system fills its own gaps without external data. The architecture forms a failure-aware sensing hierarchy, conservative when sensors work and filling in when they fail: LiDAR remains the geometric anchor, hardware depth is kept where valid, and learned depth enters only where needed. In corridor and dynamic pedestrian evaluations, selective fusion increases costmap obstacle coverage by 55-110% over LiDAR alone. A compact distilled student runs at 218\,FPS on a Jetson Orin Nano and achieves 9/10 navigation success with zero collisions in closed-loop simulation, matching the ground-truth depth baseline at a fraction of the foundation model's cost.

</details>


### [121] [Robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Small UAS Separation Assurance under GPS Degradation and Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28900)
*Alex Zongo,Filippos Fotiadis,Ufuk Topcu,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用多智能体强化学习解决小型无人机系统在GPS降级和欺骗下的鲁棒分离保障问题，通过闭式对抗扰动表达和零和博弈框架实现高效鲁棒策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协同监视中，无人机广播GPS位置信息，当这些信息被破坏时，整个空中交通状态变得不可靠。需要解决GPS降级和欺骗攻击下的安全分离保障问题。

Method: 将状态观测破坏建模为智能体与对手之间的零和博弈，推导出对抗扰动的闭式表达式，避免对抗训练，实现线性时间评估。将该对抗策略集成到MARL策略梯度算法中。

Result: 在高达35%的破坏水平下，高密度小型无人机模拟中观察到接近零的碰撞率，优于未经对抗扰动训练的基线策略。

Conclusion: 该方法为GPS降级和欺骗攻击下的小型无人机系统提供了有效的鲁棒分离保障解决方案，通过闭式对抗扰动表达实现了高效鲁棒策略学习。

Abstract: We address robust separation assurance for small Unmanned Aircraft Systems (sUAS) under GPS degradation and spoofing via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). In cooperative surveillance, each aircraft (or agent) broadcasts its GPS-derived position; when such position broadcasts are corrupted, the entire observed air traffic state becomes unreliable. We cast this state observation corruption as a zero-sum game between the agents and an adversary: with probability R, the adversary perturbs the observed state to maximally degrade each agent's safety performance. We derive a closed-form expression for this adversarial perturbation, bypassing adversarial training entirely and enabling linear-time evaluation in the state dimension. We show that this expression approximates the true worst-case adversarial perturbation with second-order accuracy. We further bound the safety performance gap between clean and corrupted observations, showing that it degrades at most linearly with the corruption probability under Kullback-Leibler regularization. Finally, we integrate the closed-form adversarial policy into a MARL policy gradient algorithm to obtain a robust counter-policy for the agents. In a high-density sUAS simulation, we observe near-zero collision rates under corruption levels up to 35%, outperforming a baseline policy trained without adversarial perturbations.

</details>


### [122] [See Something, Say Something: Context-Criticality-Aware Mobile Robot Communication for Hazard Mitigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28901)
*Bhavya Oza,Devam Shah,Ghanashyama Prabhu,Devika Kodi,Aliasghar Arab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于VLM/LLM感知的自适应消息生成框架，通过系统评估危险场景的紧急程度、时间敏感性和缓解可行性，让自主移动机器人在安全关键情况下快速响应，提高响应速度和缓解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人在安全关键场景中需要及时沟通危险，但现有系统往往响应延迟或校准不当，这会增加行动时间和损害风险。需要一种系统化的上下文敏感评估方法来减少行动时间并有效响应。

Method: 提出一个框架，利用VLM/LLM感知驱动自适应消息生成，系统评估危险场景的紧急程度、时间敏感性和缓解可行性。例如，厨房中的刀具产生平静确认，而走廊中的同一物体触发紧急协调警报。

Result: 在60多次巡逻移动机器人实验中验证，不仅实现更快响应，还将用户信任度提升至82%（相比固定优先级基线），证明结构化紧急程度评估能同时提高响应速度和缓解效果。

Conclusion: 通过VLM/LLM感知驱动的自适应消息生成框架，结合系统化的上下文敏感紧急评估，能显著提高自主移动机器人在安全关键场景中的响应速度和缓解效果，增强用户信任。

Abstract: The proverb ``see something, say something'' captures a core responsibility of autonomous mobile robots in safety-critical situations: when they detect a hazard, they must communicate--and do so quickly. In emergency scenarios, delayed or miscalibrated responses directly increase the time to action and the risk of damage. We argue that a systematic context-sensitive assessment of the criticality level, time sensitivity, and feasibility of mitigation is necessary for AMRs to reduce time to action and respond effectively. This paper presents a framework in which VLM/LLM-based perception drives adaptive message generation, for example, a knife in a kitchen produces a calm acknowledgment; the same object in a corridor triggers an urgent coordinated alert. Validation in 60+ runs using a patrolling mobile robot not only empowers faster response, but also brings user trusts to 82\% compared to fixed-priority baselines, validating that structured criticality assessment improves both response speed and mitigation effectiveness.

</details>


### [123] [Why That Robot? A Qualitative Analysis of Justification Strategies for Robot Color Selection Across Occupational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28919)
*Jiangen He,Wanqi Zhang,Jessica K. Barfield*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现用户在选择机器人时会根据肤色和拟人化特征进行选择，并合理化其选择，但实际受到社会偏见影响，特别是在不同职业背景下。研究通过分析4146条开放式解释，揭示了功能主义是主要理由，但用户会调整这些理由以符合种族和职业刻板印象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人越来越多地进入劳动力市场，需要了解隐性社会偏见如何影响用户对机器人的偏好。研究旨在探索用户如何合理化选择不同肤色和拟人化特征的机器人，以及这些选择如何在不同职业背景下受到社会偏见的影响。

Method: 通过定性分析1038名参与者的4146条开放式解释，开发并验证了一个多维编码方案（κ=0.73）。研究分析了用户在四种职业背景下选择机器人肤色的理由框架，并考察了种族刻板印象启动对颜色选择的影响。

Result: 功能主义是主要理由策略（52%），但参与者会系统性地调整这些实用理由以符合既定的种族和职业刻板印象。偏见经常在意识合理化之下运作：暴露于种族刻板印象启动显著改变了参与者的颜色选择，但他们的口头解释仍被标准情感或任务相关推理所掩盖。人口背景显著影响理由策略，机器人形状强烈调节颜色解释。当机器人高度拟人化时，用户越来越多地从功能推理转向机器中心化的去种族化。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了用户在选择机器人时如何合理化其选择，但实际受到社会偏见的影响，特别是在不同职业背景下。这些发现为设计未来劳动力机器人提供了可操作的启示，有助于减少社会偏见的延续。

Abstract: As robots increasingly enter the workforce, human-robot interaction (HRI) must address how implicit social biases influence user preferences. This paper investigates how users rationalize their selections of robots varying in skin tone and anthropomorphic features across different occupations. By qualitatively analyzing 4,146 open-ended justifications from 1,038 participants, we map the reasoning frameworks driving robot color selection across four professional contexts. We developed and validated a comprehensive, multidimensional coding scheme via human--AI consensus ($κ= 0.73$). Our results demonstrate that while utilitarian \textit{Functionalism} is the dominant justification strategy (52\%), participants systematically adapted these practical rationales to align with established racial and occupational stereotypes. Furthermore, we reveal that bias frequently operates beneath conscious rationalization: exposure to racial stereotype primes significantly shifted participants' color choices, yet their spoken justifications remained masked by standard affective or task-related reasoning. We also found that demographic backgrounds significantly shape justification strategies, and that robot shape strongly modulates color interpretation. Specifically, as robots become highly anthropomorphic, users increasingly retreat from functional reasoning toward \textit{Machine-Centric} de-racialization. Through these empirical results, we provide actionable design implications to help reduce the perpetuation of societal biases in future workforce robots.

</details>


### [124] [World2Rules: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Learning World-Governing Safety Rules for Aviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28952)
*Haichuan Wang,Jay Patrikar,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: World2Rules是一个神经符号框架，从多模态航空数据中学习安全规则，结合神经模型提取候选事实和归纳逻辑编程进行验证，在真实航空安全数据上比纯神经方法F1分数高23.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界安全关键系统依赖复杂、上下文相关的规则，手动制定不完整且容易出错。从多模态数据学习规则面临噪声、不一致和稀疏故障案例的挑战。神经模型缺乏形式化保证，符号方法对不完美观察结果脆弱。

Method: World2Rules采用神经符号框架：1) 神经模型作为候选符号事实的提议机制；2) 归纳逻辑编程作为验证层；3) 分层反射推理，在示例、子集和规则层面强制一致性；4) 过滤不可靠证据，聚合相互一致的组件，修剪不支持假设。

Result: 在真实航空安全数据上评估，World2Rules学习的规则比纯神经方法F1分数高23.6%，比单次神经符号基线高43.2%。规则紧凑、可解释，适合安全关键推理和形式化分析。

Conclusion: World2Rules通过神经符号集成和分层一致性推理，有效从噪声多模态数据中学习安全规则，限制神经提取错误传播，产生可验证、可解释的一阶逻辑规则，适用于安全关键系统。

Abstract: Many real-world safety-critical systems are governed by explicit rules that define unsafe world configurations and constrain agent interactions. In practice, these rules are complex and context-dependent, making manual specification incomplete and error-prone. Learning such rules from real-world multimodal data is further challenged by noise, inconsistency, and sparse failure cases. Neural models can extract structure from text and visual data but lack formal guarantees, while symbolic methods provide verifiability yet are brittle when applied directly to imperfect observations. We present World2Rules, a neuro-symbolic framework for learning world-governing safety rules from real-world multimodal aviation data. World2Rules learns from both nominal operational data and aviation crash and incident reports, treating neural models as proposal mechanisms for candidate symbolic facts and inductive logic programming as a verification layer. The framework employs hierarchical reflective reasoning, enforcing consistency across examples, subsets, and rules to filter unreliable evidence, aggregate only mutually consistent components, and prune unsupported hypotheses. This design limits error propagation from noisy neural extractions and yields compact, interpretable first-order logic rules that characterize unsafe world configurations. We evaluate World2Rules on real-world aviation safety data and show that it learns rules that achieve 23.6% higher F1 score than purely neural and 43.2% higher F1 score than single-pass neuro-symbolic baseline, while remaining suitable for safety-critical reasoning and formal analysis.

</details>


### [125] [AutoWorld: Scaling Multi-Agent Traffic Simulation with Self-Supervised World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28963)
*Mozhgan Pourkeshavatz,Tianran Liu,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoWorld：基于无标注激光雷达数据的交通仿真框架，通过世界模型和级联DPP采样提升仿真真实度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动仿真器依赖标注轨迹或语义标注，成本高昂难以扩展，而大量无标注传感器数据未被充分利用。需要开发能利用无标注数据提升交通仿真性能的方法。

Method: 1. 从无标注激光雷达占用表示学习世界模型；2. 构建粗到细的预测场景上下文作为多智能体运动生成模型输入；3. 使用级联Determinantal Point Process框架指导世界模型和运动模型的采样过程；4. 设计运动感知潜在监督目标增强场景动态表示。

Result: 在WOSAC基准测试中，AutoWorld在主要真实度元指标(RMM)上排名第一。实验表明，包含无标注激光雷达数据能持续提升仿真性能，各组件有效性通过消融实验验证。

Conclusion: AutoWorld为无需额外标注即可扩展交通仿真真实度开辟了新途径，通过有效利用无标注数据解决了现有方法的可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Multi-agent traffic simulation is central to developing and testing autonomous driving systems. Recent data-driven simulators have achieved promising results, but rely heavily on supervised learning from labeled trajectories or semantic annotations, making it costly to scale their performance. Meanwhile, large amounts of unlabeled sensor data can be collected at scale but remain largely unused by existing traffic simulation frameworks. This raises a key question: How can a method harness unlabeled data to improve traffic simulation performance? In this work, we propose AutoWorld, a traffic simulation framework that employs a world model learned from unlabeled occupancy representations of LiDAR data. Given world model samples, AutoWorld constructs a coarse-to-fine predictive scene context as input to a multi-agent motion generation model. To promote sample diversity, AutoWorld uses a cascaded Determinantal Point Process framework to guide the sampling processes of both the world model and the motion model. Furthermore, we designed a motion-aware latent supervision objective that enhances AutoWorld's representation of scene dynamics. Experiments on the WOSAC benchmark show that AutoWorld ranks first on the leaderboard according to the primary Realism Meta Metric (RMM). We further show that simulation performance consistently improves with the inclusion of unlabeled LiDAR data, and study the efficacy of each component with ablations. Our method paves the way for scaling traffic simulation realism without additional labeling. Our project page contains additional visualizations and released code.

</details>


### [126] [Large Neighborhood Search for Multi-Agent Task Assignment and Path Finding with Precedence Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28968)
*Viraj Parimi,Brian C. Williams*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于任务分配与路径规划问题（TAPF-PC）的大邻域搜索方法，通过迭代重新分配任务来优化解决方案质量，相比固定分配方案能显著改进89.1%的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多多机器人应用中，解决方案质量不仅取决于机器人如何移动，还取决于哪个机器人执行哪个任务。现有的MAPF-PC框架假设任务序列固定，但实际应用中需要联合优化任务分配、优先级约束满足和路径成本。

Method: 开发了一种大邻域搜索方法：从可行的MAPF-PC种子解开始，通过基于重新分配的邻域修复进行迭代改进，在每个选定的邻域内恢复可行性。

Result: 在多个基准测试族和扩展规模下的实验表明，最佳配置改进了89.1%的实例，优于固定分配的种子解决方案，证明大邻域搜索能有效捕捉在优先级约束下灵活重新分配的收益。

Conclusion: 大邻域搜索方法能有效解决任务分配与路径规划问题（TAPF-PC），通过联合优化任务分配和路径规划，在满足优先级约束的同时显著提升解决方案质量。

Abstract: Many multi-robot applications require tasks to be completed efficiently and in the correct order, so that downstream operations can proceed at the right time. Multi-agent path finding with precedence constraints (MAPF-PC) is a well-studied framework for computing collision-free plans that satisfy ordering relations when task sequences are fixed in advance. In many applications, however, solution quality depends not only on how agents move, but also on which agent performs which task. This motivates the lifted problem of task assignment and path finding with precedence constraints (TAPF-PC), which extends MAPF-PC by jointly optimizing assignment, precedence satisfaction, and routing cost. To address the resulting coupled TAPF-PC search space, we develop a large neighborhood search approach that starts from a feasible MAPF-PC seed and iteratively improves it through reassignment-based neighborhood repair, restoring feasibility within each selected neighborhood. Experiments across multiple benchmark families and scaling regimes show that the best-performing configuration improves 89.1% of instances over fixed-assignment seed solutions, demonstrating that large neighborhood search effectively captures the gains from flexible reassignment under precedence constraints.

</details>


### [127] [Gleanmer: A 6 mW SoC for Real-Time 3D Gaussian Occupancy Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29005)
*Zih-Sing Fu,Peter Zhi Xuan Li,Sertac Karaman,Vivienne Sze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Gleanmer是一个用于3D占用映射的SoC芯片，通过算法-硬件协同优化，在边缘设备上实现实时低功耗的3D高斯映射


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘应用（如AR/VR和自主导航）需要高保真3D占用映射，但受到功率限制的制约，需要低功耗的实时解决方案

Method: 提出Gleanmer SoC芯片，采用GMMap（基于高斯的3D占用映射）算法，通过算法-硬件协同优化实现直接计算和高效重用紧凑高斯模型，并使用近似计算技术

Result: 地图构建和查询能耗分别降低63%和81%，加速器面积减少38%，在16nm CMOS工艺下实时处理640x480图像（超过88fps），查询时每秒处理超过540K坐标，总功耗低于6mW

Conclusion: Gleanmer是首个在边缘应用中实现实时3D占用映射且功耗低于6mW的SoC芯片，为AR/VR和自主导航等应用提供了可行的低功耗解决方案

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D occupancy mapping is essential for many edge-based applications (such as AR/VR and autonomous navigation) but is limited by power constraints. We present Gleanmer, a system on chip (SoC) with an accelerator for GMMap, a 3D occupancy map using Gaussians. Through algorithm-hardware co-optimizations for direct computation and efficient reuse of these compact Gaussians, Gleanmer reduces construction and query energy by up to 63% and 81%, respectively. Approximate computation on Gaussians reduces accelerator area by 38%. Using 16nm CMOS, Gleanmer processes 640x480 images in real time beyond 88 fps during map construction and processes over 540K coordinates per second during map query. To our knowledge, Gleanmer is the first fabricated SoC to achieve real-time 3D occupancy mapping under 6 mW for edge-based applications.

</details>


### [128] [Efficient Camera Pose Augmentation for View Generalization in Robotic Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29192)
*Sen Wang,Huaiyi Dong,Jingyi Tian,Jiayi Li,Zhuo Yang,Tongtong Cao,Anlin Chen,Shuang Wu,Le Wang,Sanping Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenSplat：基于3D高斯泼溅的视角泛化策略学习框架，通过单次前向重建3D场景并渲染新视角来增强策略的鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D视觉运动策略在未见视角下泛化能力差，因为它们依赖静态观测，无法在不同视角下保持一致的行动映射

Method: 1) 使用置换等变架构从稀疏未标定输入单次前向重建3D场景；2) 设计3D先验蒸馏策略正则化3DGS优化，防止几何坍塌；3) 从稳定3D表示渲染多样合成视角，系统性地增强训练观测流形

Result: 该方法迫使策略基于底层3D结构做出决策，在严重空间扰动下实现鲁棒执行，而基线方法性能严重下降

Conclusion: GenSplat通过3D场景重建和新视角渲染，有效解决了2D策略的视角泛化问题，提升了策略在空间扰动下的鲁棒性

Abstract: Prevailing 2D-centric visuomotor policies exhibit a pronounced deficiency in novel view generalization, as their reliance on static observations hinders consistent action mapping across unseen views. In response, we introduce GenSplat, a feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting framework that facilitates view-generalized policy learning through novel view rendering. GenSplat employs a permutation-equivariant architecture to reconstruct high-fidelity 3D scenes from sparse, uncalibrated inputs in a single forward pass. To ensure structural integrity, we design a 3D-prior distillation strategy that regularizes the 3DGS optimization, preventing the geometric collapse typical of purely photometric supervision. By rendering diverse synthetic views from these stable 3D representations, we systematically augment the observational manifold during training. This augmentation forces the policy to ground its decisions in underlying 3D structures, thereby ensuring robust execution under severe spatial perturbations where baselines severely degrade.

</details>


### [129] [Kilohertz-Safe: A Scalable Framework for Constrained Dexterous Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29213)
*Yinxiao Tian,Ziyi Yang,Zinan Zhao,Zhen Kan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个可扩展的运动重定向框架，将非线性重定向问题转化为凸二次规划问题，实现高频率实时性能并保证安全性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手遥操作需要同时实现高频实时性能和异构运动学与安全约束的运动重定向方法。现有非线性优化方法计算成本高，难以应用于千赫兹级控制，而学习方法通常缺乏正式的安全保证。

Method: 将非线性重定向问题重新表述为关节微分空间中的凸二次规划问题。通过系统线性化纳入异构约束（包括运动学限制和碰撞避免），并集成控制屏障函数提供正式安全保证。

Result: 在Wuji Hand平台上通过仿真和硬件实验验证，优于Dex-Retargeting和GeoRT等最先进方法。平均延迟为9.05毫秒，超过95%的重定向帧满足安全标准，有效减少了复杂操作任务中的自碰撞和穿透。

Conclusion: 提出的框架实现了高频率操作和正式安全保证，为灵巧手遥操作提供了一种高效可靠的运动重定向解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous hand teleoperation requires motion re-targeting methods that simultaneously achieve high-frequency real-time performance and enforcement of heterogeneous kinematic and safety constraints. Existing nonlinear optimization-based approaches often incur prohibitive computational cost, limiting their applicability to kilohertz-level control, while learning-based methods typically lack formal safety guarantees. This paper proposes a scalable motion retargeting framework that reformulates the nonlinear retargeting problem into a convex quadratic program in joint differential space. Heterogeneous constraints, including kinematic limits and collision avoidance, are incorporated through systematic linearization, resulting in improved computational efficiency and numerical stability. Control barrier functions are further integrated to provide formal safety guarantees during the retargeting process. The proposed framework is validated through simulations and hardware experiments on the Wuji Hand platform, outperforming state-of-the-art methods such as Dex-Retargeting and GeoRT. The framework achieves high-frequency operation with an average latency of 9.05 ms, while over 95% of retargeted frames satisfy the safety criteria, effectively mitigating self-collision and penetration during complex manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [130] [Long-Reach Robotic Manipulation for Assembly and Outfitting of Lunar Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29226)
*Stanley Wang,Venny Kojouharov,Long Yin Chung,Daniel Morton,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于月球基础设施建设的紧凑型长臂机械手，采用可展开复合臂，能够在大型结构上执行电缆布线等精细操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球表面未来基础设施建设需要半自主或全自主机器人操作，特别是电缆布线等任务需要在大型结构上进行运输、布线和精细操作，需要长臂机械手来满足这一需求。

Method: 设计了一种结合可展开复合臂的紧凑型长臂机械手，分析了可展开结构的偏转、振动和展开特性，并提出了相应的操作控制策略来减轻这些影响。

Result: 实验表明，在臂长达到1.8米时，末端精度误差平均小于15毫米。通过电缆布线任务演示了该技术在月球装备应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: 该可展开复合臂机械手能够满足月球基础设施建设中对长臂操作的需求，特别是在电缆布线等精细操作任务中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Future infrastructure construction on the lunar surface will require semi- or fully-autonomous operation from robots deployed at the build site. In particular, tasks such as electrical outfitting necessitate transport, routing, and fine manipulation of cables across large structures. To address this need, we present a compact and long-reach manipulator incorporating a deployable composite boom, capable of performing manipulation tasks across large structures and workspaces. We characterize the deflection, vibration, and blossoming characteristics inherent to the deployable structure, and present a manipulation control strategy to mitigate these effects. Experiments indicate an average endpoint accuracy error of less than 15 mm for boom lengths up to 1.8 m. We demonstrate the approach with a cable routing task to illustrate the potential for lunar outfitting applications that benefit from long reach.

</details>


### [131] [Kernel-SDF: An Open-Source Library for Real-Time Signed Distance Function Estimation using Kernel Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29227)
*Zhirui Dai,Tianxing Fan,Mani Amani,Jaemin Seo,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Hyondong Oh,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Kernel-SDF：一个基于核回归的开源库，用于从流式传感器数据中实时学习带校准不确定性的符号距离函数，优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SDF重建方法在大规模不确定性感知的SDF估计方面存在局限：体素方法受固定分辨率限制且缺乏不确定性量化，神经网络方法训练时间长，高斯过程方法存在可扩展性、符号估计和不确定性校准问题

Method: 使用核回归学习SDF：前端通过核回归学习连续占据场，后端使用从前端表面边界采样的数据通过高斯过程回归估计精确SDF，提供实时SDF、SDF梯度、SDF不确定性和网格构建

Result: Kernel-SDF在保持实时性能的同时，相比现有方法实现了更高的精度，适用于需要可靠不确定性感知几何信息的各种机器人应用

Conclusion: Kernel-SDF是一个有效的开源解决方案，能够从流式传感器数据中实时学习带校准不确定性的SDF，解决了现有方法的局限性，为机器人运动规划、操作和导航等应用提供了可靠的环境表示

Abstract: Accurate and efficient environment representation is crucial for robotic applications such as motion planning, manipulation, and navigation. Signed distance functions (SDFs) have emerged as a powerful representation for encoding distance to obstacle boundaries, enabling efficient collision-checking and trajectory optimization techniques. However, existing SDF reconstruction methods have limitations when it comes to large-scale uncertainty-aware SDF estimation from streaming sensor data. Voxel-based approaches are limited by fixed resolution and lack uncertainty quantification, neural network methods require significant training time, while Gaussian process (GP) methods struggle with scalability, sign estimation, and uncertainty calibration. In this letter, we develop an open-source library, Kernel-SDF, which uses kernel regression to learn SDF with calibrated uncertainty quantification in real-time. Our approach consists of a front-end that learns a continuous occupancy field via kernel regression, and a back-end that estimates accurate SDF via GP regression using samples from the front-end surface boundaries. Kernel-SDF provides accurate SDF, SDF gradient, SDF uncertainty, and mesh construction in real-time. Evaluation results show that Kernel-SDF achieves superior accuracy compared to existing methods, while maintaining real-time performance, making it suitable for various robotics applications requiring reliable uncertainty-aware geometric information.

</details>


### [132] [Long-Reach Robotic Cleaning for Lunar Solar Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29240)
*Stanley Wang,Velin Kojouharov,Long Yin Chung,Daniel Morton,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于月球太阳能板清洁的小型移动机器人，配备长臂机械臂和可更换清洁工具，通过柔顺腕部和力控制实现稳定接触清洁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球商业活动加速需要可靠的表面基础设施维护。太阳能板作为主要电力来源，长期运行中会积累月尘导致性能下降，需要清洁维护以延长任务寿命。

Method: 开发小型移动机器人，配备轻质可展开长臂机械臂和可更换清洁工具。在先前视觉引导长臂操作基础上，增加带远端力感知的柔顺腕部和基于速度的导纳控制器，以调节清洁过程中的稳定接触。

Result: 在平面工作台的初步实验中，系统在0.3米到1.0米臂长范围内执行简单清洁运动时，能维持约2N的法向力，初始接触后的RMS力误差约为0.2N。

Conclusion: 可展开长臂机械臂是用于月球基础设施（如太阳能板、散热器和光学表面）机器人维护的有前景架构，早期结果表明其可行性。

Abstract: Commercial lunar activity is accelerating the need for reliable surface infrastructure and routine operations to keep it functioning. Maintenance tasks such as inspection, cleaning, dust mitigation, and minor repair are essential to preserve performance and extend system life. A specific application is the cleaning of lunar solar arrays. Solar arrays are expected to provide substantial fraction of lunar surface power and operate for months to years, supplying continuous energy to landers, habitats, and surface assets, making sustained output mission-critical. However, over time lunar dust accumulates on these large solar arrays, which can rapidly degrade panel output and reduce mission lifetime. We propose a small mobile robot equipped with a long-reach, lightweight deployable boom and interchangeable cleaning tool to perform gentle cleaning over meter-scale workspaces with minimal human involvement. Building on prior vision-guided long-reach manipulation, we add a compliant wrist with distal force sensing and a velocity-based admittance controller to regulate stable contact during surface cleaning. In preliminary benchtop experiments on a planar surface, the system maintained approximately 2 N normal force while executing a simple cleaning motion over boom lengths from 0.3 m to 1.0 m, with RMS force error of approximately 0.2 N after initial contact. These early results suggest that deployable long-reach manipulators are a promising architecture for robotic maintenance of lunar infrastructure such as solar arrays, radiators, and optical surfaces.

</details>


### [133] [SuperGrasp: Single-View Object Grasping via Superquadric Similarity Matching, Evaluation, and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29254)
*Lijingze Xiao,Jinhong Du,Yang Cong,Supeng Diao,Yu Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuperGrasp：一个用于单视角抓取的两阶段框架，通过相似性匹配检索抓取候选，再通过E-RNet网络进行评估和优化，在仿真和真实环境中都表现出稳定的抓取性能和强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视角观测下的机器人抓取仍然是一个关键挑战，现有方法在面对不完整几何信息时难以生成稳定有效的抓取姿态。需要解决单视角抓取中几何信息不完整的问题。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段使用相似性匹配模块，通过超二次曲面系数匹配输入点云与预计算基元数据集来检索抓取候选；第二阶段提出E-RNet网络，扩展抓取感知区域，以初始抓取闭合区域作为局部锚点区域，进行更准确可靠的评估和优化。

Result: 构建了包含1.5k个基元的相似性匹配数据集，以及包含124个物体10万个稳定抓取标签的大规模点云数据集用于网络训练。在仿真和真实环境中的广泛实验表明，该方法在不同场景和新物体上实现了稳定的抓取性能和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: SuperGrasp框架通过分解抓取过程为初始姿态生成和后续评估优化，有效解决了单视角抓取中几何信息不完整的问题，实现了稳定抓取和强泛化能力。

Abstract: Robotic grasping from single-view observations remains a critical challenge in manipulation. Existing methods still struggle to generate stable and valid grasp poses when confronted with incomplete geometric information. To address these limitations, we propose SuperGrasp, a novel two-stage framework for single-view grasping with parallel-jaw grippers that decomposes the grasping process into initial grasp pose generation and subsequent grasp evaluation and refinement. In the first stage, we introduce a Similarity Matching Module that efficiently retrieves grasp candidates by matching the input single-view point cloud with a pre-computed primitive dataset based on superquadric coefficients. In the second stage, we propose E-RNet, an end-to-end network that expands the graspaware region and takes the initial grasp closure region as a local anchor region, enabling more accurate and reliable evaluation and refinement of grasp candidates. To enhance generalization, we construct a primitive dataset containing 1.5k primitives for similarity matching and collect a large-scale point cloud dataset with 100k stable grasp labels from 124 objects for network training. Extensive experiments in both simulation and realworld environments demonstrate that our method achieves stable grasping performance and strong generalization across varying scenes and novel objects.

</details>


### [134] [IMPASTO: Integrating Model-Based Planning with Learned Dynamics Models for Robotic Oil Painting Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29315)
*Yingke Wang,Hao Li,Yifeng Zhu,Hong-Xing Yu,Ken Goldberg,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Yunzhu Li,Ruohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMPASTO是一个机器人油画系统，通过自学习像素动态模型和基于模型的规划，能够仅根据目标油画图像序列推断并执行笔触轨迹、力度和颜色，实现高质量的油画复制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人复制油画需要控制可变形工具、预测笔触效果并进行多步骤规划，但通常缺乏人类逐步演示或精确模拟器。研究旨在探索机器人能否仅从目标油画图像序列中推断并执行所需的笔触轨迹、力度和颜色。

Method: IMPASTO系统整合了学习的像素动态模型与基于模型的规划：动态模型从图像观察和参数化笔触动作预测画布更新；模型预测控制优化器规划轨迹和力度；力敏感控制器在7自由度机械臂上执行笔触。系统仅通过机器人自学习训练。

Result: IMPASTO能够近似人类艺术家的单笔触数据集和多笔触艺术作品，在复制准确性方面优于基线方法，展示了从自学习中实现高质量油画复制的能力。

Conclusion: IMPASTO成功整合了低层力控制、学习动态模型和高层闭环规划，仅通过机器人自学习就能实现油画复制，为机器人艺术创作提供了新方法。

Abstract: Robotic reproduction of oil paintings using soft brushes and pigments requires force-sensitive control of deformable tools, prediction of brushstroke effects, and multi-step stroke planning, often without human step-by-step demonstrations or faithful simulators. Given only a sequence of target oil painting images, can a robot infer and execute the stroke trajectories, forces, and colors needed to reproduce it? We present IMPASTO, a robotic oil-painting system that integrates learned pixel dynamics models with model-based planning. The dynamics models predict canvas updates from image observations and parameterized stroke actions; a receding-horizon model predictive control optimizer then plans trajectories and forces, while a force-sensitive controller executes strokes on a 7-DoF robot arm. IMPASTO integrates low-level force control, learned dynamics models, and high-level closed-loop planning, learns solely from robot self-play, and approximates human artists' single-stroke datasets and multi-stroke artworks, outperforming baselines in reproduction accuracy. Project website: https://impasto-robopainting.github.io/

</details>


### [135] [Scaling Whole-Body Human Musculoskeletal Behavior Emulation for Specificity and Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29332)
*Yunyue Wei,Chenhui Zuo,Shanning Zhuang,Haixin Gong,Yaming Liu,Yanan Sui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MS-Emulator框架通过大规模并行GPU模拟、对抗性奖励聚合和值引导流探索，解决了高维肌肉骨骼控制中的优化瓶颈，能够准确复现舞蹈、侧手翻、后空翻等动态动作，并探索了肌肉骨骼控制策略的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动控制的具身学习需要全身神经驱动的肌肉骨骼动力学，但内部肌肉驱动过程无法直接测量。传统计算方法存在局限性：逆动力学方法难以从观测运动学中解析高维过驱动系统的冗余控制；基于深度强化学习的前向模仿方法因控制和奖励设计的维度灾难而跟踪性能不足。

Method: 提出MS-Emulator框架，整合大规模并行GPU模拟、对抗性奖励聚合和值引导流探索，克服高维强化学习在肌肉骨骼控制中的优化瓶颈。该框架用于约700块肌肉驱动的全身人体肌肉骨骼系统。

Result: 准确复现了舞蹈、侧手翻、后空翻等多种动态动作，实现了高关节角度精度和身体位置对齐。还探索了肌肉骨骼控制解空间，发现了收敛到几乎相同外部运动学和力学测量的不同控制策略。

Conclusion: 这项工作为分析人类运动控制的具体性和多样性建立了一条可行的计算途径，能够解析肌肉骨骼控制策略的多样性和特异性。

Abstract: The embodied learning of human motor control requires whole-body neuro-actuated musculoskeletal dynamics, while the internal muscle-driven processes underlying movement remain inaccessible to direct measurement. Computational modeling offers an alternative, but inverse dynamics methods struggled to resolve redundant control from observed kinematics in the high-dimensional, over-actuated system. Forward imitation approaches based on deep reinforcement learning exhibited inadequate tracking performance due to the curse of dimensionality in both control and reward design. Here we introduce a large-scale parallel musculoskeletal computation framework for biomechanically grounded whole-body motion reproduction. By integrating large-scale parallel GPU simulation with adversarial reward aggregation and value-guided flow exploration, the MS-Emulator framework overcomes key optimization bottlenecks in high-dimensional reinforcement learning for musculoskeletal control, which accurately reproduces a broad repertoire of motions in a whole-body human musculoskeletal system actuated by approximately 700 muscles. It achieved high joint angle accuracy and body position alignment for highly dynamic tasks such as dance, cartwheel, and backflip. The framework was also used to explore the musculoskeletal control solution space, identifying distinct musculoskeletal control policies that converge to nearly identical external kinematic and mechanical measurements. This work establishes a tractable computational route to analyzing the specificity and diversity underlying human embodied control of movement. Project page: https://lnsgroup.cc/research/MS-Emulator.

</details>


### [136] [Industrial-Grade Robust Robot Vision for Screw Detection and Removal under Uneven Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29363)
*Tomoki Ishikura,Genichiro Matsuda,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自动化空调室外机拆卸系统，结合两阶段检测和局部校准策略，在真实测试中达到78.3%拆卸成功率和193秒平均周期时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日本劳动力减少而废旧家电增加，需要自动化回收流程。空调室外机拆卸自动化面临挑战：尺寸变化大、污垢锈蚀严重。

Method: 集成任务特定的两阶段检测方法和基于网格的局部校准策略，无需预编程坐标，提高螺丝检测精度和操作准确性。

Result: 螺丝检测召回率达99.8%（即使严重退化），操作精度±0.75mm。在120台真实测试中，拆卸成功率78.3%，平均周期时间193秒。

Conclusion: 系统验证了工业应用的可行性，为家电回收自动化提供了有效解决方案，能够应对实际环境中的尺寸变化和退化问题。

Abstract: As the amount of used home appliances is expected to increase despite the decreasing labor force in Japan, there is a need to automate disassembling processes at recycling plants. The automation of disassembling air conditioner outdoor units, however, remains a challenge due to unit size variations and exposure to dirt and rust. To address these challenges, this study proposes an automated system that integrates a task-specific two-stage detection method and a lattice-based local calibration strategy. This approach achieved a screw detection recall of 99.8% despite severe degradation and ensured a manipulation accuracy of +/-0.75 mm without pre-programmed coordinates. In real-world validation with 120 units, the system attained a disassembly success rate of 78.3% and an average cycle time of 193 seconds, confirming its feasibility for industrial application.

</details>


### [137] [Interacting Multiple Model Proprioceptive Odometry for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29383)
*Wanlei Li,Zichang Chen,Shilei Li,Xiaogang Xiong,Yunjiang Lou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于交互多模型(IMM)的足式机器人本体感知里程计框架，通过多接触假设解决传统点接触假设在实际运动中的局限性，提升姿态估计精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人状态估计面临挑战，本体感知里程计因可观测性有限而依赖测量约束抑制漂移。当外部传感器不可靠时，主要依赖本体感知测量，特别是接触相关的腿部运动学信息。然而，现有方法依赖理想化的点接触假设，在实际运动中常被违反，导致约束效果降低和估计精度下降

Method: 提出基于交互多模型(IMM)的本体感知里程计框架，在统一概率框架中整合多种接触假设，实现在线模式切换和不同接触条件下的概率融合

Result: 广泛的仿真和真实世界实验表明，该方法在姿态估计精度上优于现有最先进方法，同时保持相当的计算效率

Conclusion: 提出的IMM框架有效解决了足式机器人本体感知里程计中接触假设的理想化问题，通过多接触假设的概率融合提升了在不同接触条件下的鲁棒性和估计精度

Abstract: State estimation for legged robots remains challenging because legged odometry generally suffers from limited observability and therefore depends critically on measurement constraints to suppress drift. When exteroceptive sensors are unreliable or degraded, such constraints are mainly derived from proprioceptive measurements, particularly contact-related leg kinematics information. However, most existing proprioceptive odometry methods rely on an idealized point-contact assumption, which is often violated during real locomotion. Consequently, the effectiveness of proprioceptive constraints may be significantly reduced, resulting in degraded estimation accuracy. To address these limitations, we propose an interacting multiple model (IMM)-based proprioceptive odometry framework for legged robots. By incorporating multiple contact hypotheses within a unified probabilistic framework, the proposed method enables online mode switching and probabilistic fusion under varying contact conditions. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior pose estimation accuracy over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining comparable computational efficiency.

</details>


### [138] [Learning Semantic Priorities for Autonomous Target Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29391)
*Max Lodel,Nils Wilde,Robert Babuška,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用专家输入训练语义优先级模型的目标搜索方法，通过组合优化在边界探索规划器中使用学习到的优先级，实现基于语义特征的高效目标搜索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前目标搜索方法依赖相似领域的大数据集训练，限制了在多样化环境中的适应性。人类专家拥有关于语义关系的高层知识，可以有效指导机器人在多样化且未见过的环境中进行目标搜索

Method: 利用专家输入训练语义优先级模型，将学习到的优先级应用于基于组合优化的边界探索规划器，通过模拟专家指导的合成数据集训练语义优先级模型

Result: 在未见过的环境中进行模拟测试，该方法始终比覆盖驱动探索规划器实现更快的目标恢复

Conclusion: 提出的方法通过结合专家知识和语义优先级模型，实现了在多样化环境中高效、鲁棒且完全覆盖的目标搜索

Abstract: The use of semantic features can improve the efficiency of target search in unknown environments for robotic search and rescue missions. Current target search methods rely on training with large datasets of similar domains, which limits the adaptability to diverse environments. However, human experts possess high-level knowledge about semantic relationships necessary to effectively guide a robot during target search missions in diverse and previously unseen environments. In this paper, we propose a target search method that leverages expert input to train a model of semantic priorities. By employing the learned priorities in a frontier exploration planner using combinatorial optimization, our approach achieves efficient target search driven by semantic features while ensuring robustness and complete coverage. The proposed semantic priority model is trained with several synthetic datasets of simulated expert guidance for target search. Simulation tests in previously unseen environments show that our method consistently achieves faster target recovery than a coverage-driven exploration planner.

</details>


### [139] [CLaD: Planning with Grounded Foresight via Cross-Modal Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29409)
*Andrew Jeong,Jaemin Kim,Sebin Lee,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLaD框架通过非对称跨注意力机制联合建模本体感知和语义状态在动作下的演化，预测基础潜在前瞻，并用扩散策略生成动作，在LIBERO-LONG基准上达到94.7%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在语义空间或潜在空间中规划，但没有显式对齐跨模态的过渡。机器人操作涉及通过底层动作耦合的动力学和语义过渡，需要联合建模这两种状态的演化。

Method: 提出CLaD框架：1) 使用非对称跨注意力让动力学过渡查询语义过渡；2) 通过自监督目标和EMA目标编码器预测基础潜在前瞻；3) 用辅助重建损失防止表示坍塌；4) 用观测调制预测的前瞻，条件化扩散策略生成动作。

Result: 在LIBERO-LONG基准上达到94.7%的成功率，与参数更多的大型视觉语言动作模型（VLA）性能相当，但参数数量显著减少。

Conclusion: CLaD通过显式对齐跨模态过渡，有效建模了本体感知和语义状态的联合演化，在机器人操作任务上取得了高性能，同时保持了模型的紧凑性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation involves kinematic and semantic transitions that are inherently coupled via underlying actions. However, existing approaches plan within either semantic or latent space without explicitly aligning these cross-modal transitions. To address this, we propose CLaD, a framework that models how proprioceptive and semantic states jointly evolve under actions through asymmetric cross-attention that allows kinematic transitions to query semantic ones. CLaD predicts grounded latent foresights via self-supervised objectives with EMA target encoders and auxiliary reconstruction losses, preventing representation collapse while anchoring predictions to observable states. Predicted foresights are modulated with observations to condition a diffusion policy for action generation. On LIBERO-LONG benchmark, CLaD achieves 94.7\% success rate, competitive with large VLAs with significantly fewer parameters.

</details>


### [140] [RAAP: Retrieval-Augmented Affordance Prediction with Cross-Image Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29419)
*Qiyuan Zhuang,He-Yang Xu,Yijun Wang,Xin-Yang Zhao,Yang-Yang Li,Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAAP是一个检索增强的物体可操作性预测框架，通过分离静态接触点定位和动态动作方向预测，结合密集对应和双权重注意力机制，实现对新物体和类别的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体可操作性预测方法存在局限性：检索方法因稀疏性和覆盖不足而脆弱，大规模模型在处理未见类别时经常错误定位接触点并误预测接触后动作，阻碍了鲁棒泛化。

Method: RAAP统一了可操作性检索和基于对齐的学习。通过解耦静态接触点定位（通过密集对应传递）和动态动作方向预测（通过检索增强对齐模型，使用双权重注意力整合多个参考）。

Result: 在DROID和HOI4D的紧凑子集上训练（每个任务仅需数十个样本），RAAP在未见物体和类别上表现一致，并在仿真和真实世界中实现零样本机器人操作。

Conclusion: RAAP通过结合检索和基于对齐的学习，解决了现有方法的局限性，实现了鲁棒的零样本泛化，为机器人在非结构化环境中的精细交互提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Understanding object affordances is essential for enabling robots to perform purposeful and fine-grained interactions in diverse and unstructured environments. However, existing approaches either rely on retrieval, which is fragile due to sparsity and coverage gaps, or on large-scale models, which frequently mislocalize contact points and mispredict post-contact actions when applied to unseen categories, thereby hindering robust generalization. We introduce Retrieval-Augmented Affordance Prediction (RAAP), a framework that unifies affordance retrieval with alignment-based learning. By decoupling static contact localization and dynamic action direction, RAAP transfers contact points via dense correspondence and predicts action directions through a retrieval-augmented alignment model that consolidates multiple references with dual-weighted attention. Trained on compact subsets of DROID and HOI4D with as few as tens of samples per task, RAAP achieves consistent performance across unseen objects and categories, and enables zero-shot robotic manipulation in both simulation and the real world. Project website: https://github.com/SEU-VIPGroup/RAAP.

</details>


### [141] [CReF: Cross-modal and Recurrent Fusion for Depth-conditioned Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29452)
*Yuan Hao,Ruiqi Yu,Shixin Luo,Guoteng Zhang,Jun Wu,Qiuguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CReF框架，通过跨模态注意力直接从未处理的前向深度数据学习人形机器人运动特征，无需几何中间表示，结合地形感知落脚点奖励，实现复杂地形鲁棒穿越


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知人形机器人运动方法依赖显式几何抽象（如2.5D地形表示或几何相关监督目标），在处理垂直结构、穿孔障碍和复杂现实杂乱环境时存在限制

Method: CReF框架：1) 通过本体感知查询的跨模态注意力耦合本体感知和深度标记；2) 门控残差融合块融合表示；3) GRU进行时间集成，使用高速公路式输出门实现状态依赖的特征混合；4) 地形感知落脚点放置奖励从足端点云提取可支撑落脚点候选

Result: 在仿真和物理人形机器人实验中，实现了对多样化地形的鲁棒穿越，并有效零样本迁移到包含扶手、空心托盘组装、严重反射干扰和视觉杂乱户外环境的真实场景

Conclusion: CReF框架通过直接从未处理深度数据学习运动相关特征，避免了中间几何表示的偏差，实现了对复杂几何地形更灵活和鲁棒的穿越能力

Abstract: Stable traversal over geometrically complex terrain increasingly requires exteroceptive perception, yet prior perceptive humanoid locomotion methods often remain tied to explicit geometric abstractions, either by mediating control through robot-centric 2.5D terrain representations or by shaping depth learning with auxiliary geometry-related targets. Such designs inherit the representational bias of the intermediate or supervisory target and can be restrictive for vertical structures, perforated obstacles, and complex real-world clutter. We propose CReF (Cross-modal and Recurrent Fusion), a single-stage depth-conditioned humanoid locomotion framework that learns locomotion-relevant features directly from raw forward-facing depth without explicit geometric intermediates. CReF couples proprioception and depth tokens through proprioception-queried cross-modal attention, fuses the resulting representation with a gated residual fusion block, and performs temporal integration with a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU) regulated by a highway-style output gate for state-dependent blending of recurrent and feedforward features. To further improve terrain interaction, we introduce a terrain-aware foothold placement reward that extracts supportable foothold candidates from foot-end point-cloud samples and rewards touchdown locations that lie close to the nearest supportable candidate. Experiments in simulation and on a physical humanoid demonstrate robust traversal over diverse terrains and effective zero-shot transfer to real-world scenes containing handrails, hollow pallet assemblies, severe reflective interference, and visually cluttered outdoor surroundings.

</details>


### [142] [Communication Outage-Resistant UUV State Estimation: A Variational History Distillation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29512)
*Shuyue Li,Miguel López-Benítez,Eng Gee Lim,Fei Ma,Qian Dong,Mengze Cao,Limin Yu,Xiaohui Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出变分历史蒸馏方法，通过从历史轨迹提取模式合成虚拟测量，在UUV集群通信中断时显著降低状态估计误差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人水下航行器集群依赖连续声学通信，但通信易受间歇中断。通信中断时，标准状态估计器（如UKF）被迫进行开环预测，若环境中存在未建模动态因素（如未知洋流），估计误差会迅速增长，可能导致任务失败。

Method: 提出变分历史蒸馏方法，将轨迹预测视为近似贝叶斯推理过程，将基于物理的标准运动模型与直接从UUV历史轨迹提取的模式相结合。通过从历史轨迹蒸馏出"虚拟测量"来实现。引入自适应置信机制，随着通信中断时间延长，滤波器逐渐降低对虚拟测量的信任度。

Result: 在高保真环境中的大量蒙特卡洛模拟表明，该方法在40秒通信中断期间将预测均方根误差降低了91%，从约170米减少到15米。

Conclusion: VHD方法即使在完全通信丢失的情况下也能保持鲁棒的状态估计性能，显著提高了UUV集群在通信中断时的可靠运行能力。

Abstract: The reliable operation of Unmanned Underwater Vehicle (UUV) clusters is highly dependent on continuous acoustic communication. However, this communication method is highly susceptible to intermittent interruptions. When communication outages occur, standard state estimators such as the Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) will be forced to make open-loop predictions. If the environment contains unmodeled dynamic factors, such as unknown ocean currents, this estimation error will grow rapidly, which may eventually lead to mission failure. To address this critical issue, this paper proposes a Variational History Distillation (VHD) approach. VHD regards trajectory prediction as an approximate Bayesian reasoning process, which links a standard motion model based on physics with a pattern extracted directly from the past trajectory of the UUV. This is achieved by synthesizing ``virtual measurements'' distilled from historical trajectories. Recognizing that the reliability of extrapolated historical trends degrades over extended prediction horizons, an adaptive confidence mechanism is introduced. This mechanism allows the filter to gradually reduce the trust of virtual measurements as the communication outage time is extended. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations in a high-fidelity environment demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a 91\% reduction in prediction Root Mean Square Error (RMSE), reducing the error from approximately 170 m to 15 m during a 40-second communication outage. These results demonstrate that VHD can maintain robust state estimation performance even under complete communication loss.

</details>


### [143] [GraSP-STL: A Graph-Based Framework for Zero-Shot Signal Temporal Logic Planning via Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29533)
*Ancheng Hou,Ruijia Liu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GraSP-STL框架，基于离线轨迹进行零样本STL规划，无需环境交互或任务特定训练


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离线、零样本规划问题：只有任务无关行为策略收集的离线数据集，没有解析动力学模型、环境交互或任务特定重训练，需要合成满足任意未见STL规范的控制策略

Method: GraSP-STL框架：1) 从离线数据学习目标条件值函数，导出有限时域可达性度量；2) 构建有向图抽象，节点代表代表性状态，边编码可行短时域转移；3) 将规划公式化为图搜索，使用算术几何平均鲁棒度及其区间语义评估，通过学习的目标条件策略执行

Result: 实验结果表明该框架在一系列离线STL规划任务中有效

Conclusion: 提出的框架将可重用的可达性学习与任务条件规划分离，实现了对未见STL任务的零样本泛化，并通过离线数据的短时域行为组合实现长时域规划

Abstract: This paper studies offline, zero-shot planning under Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications. We assume access only to an offline dataset of state-action-state transitions collected by a task-agnostic behavior policy, with no analytical dynamics model, no further environment interaction, and no task-specific retraining. The objective is to synthesize a control strategy whose resulting trajectory satisfies an arbitrary unseen STL specification. To this end, we propose GraSP-STL, a graph-search-based framework for zero-shot STL planning from offline trajectories. The method learns a goal-conditioned value function from offline data and uses it to induce a finite-horizon reachability metric over the state space. Based on this metric, it constructs a directed graph abstraction whose nodes represent representative states and whose edges encode feasible short-horizon transitions. Planning is then formulated as a graph search over waypoint sequences, evaluated using arithmetic-geometric mean robustness and its interval semantics, and executed by a learned goal-conditioned policy. The proposed framework separates reusable reachability learning from task-conditioned planning, enabling zero-shot generalization to unseen STL tasks and long-horizon planning through the composition of short-horizon behaviors from offline data. Experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness on a range of offline STL planning tasks.

</details>


### [144] [Semantic Zone-Based Map Management for Stable AI-Integrated Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29627)
*Huichang Yun,Seungho Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于语义区域的地图管理方法，在内存受限的移动机器人上稳定使用稠密地图和大型AI模型，通过语义区域级的关键帧管理减少加载/卸载频率，提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人需要同时运行大型AI模型（VLMs/LLMs）和使用稠密地图，但在边缘设备上受内存限制。内存不足时关键帧无法及时加载，会影响定位稳定性和模型性能

Method: 基于语义区域的地图管理方法：将关键帧与室内语义区域（房间、走廊等）关联，在语义区域级别进行关键帧管理，优先加载空间相关的地图内容，同时遵守内存约束

Result: 在大型模拟室内环境和NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上评估，使用Qwen3.5:0.8b模型时，相比几何地图管理策略，吞吐量提升3.3 tokens/s，延迟降低21.7%。消除了内存不足导致的失败和执行停滞，保持了定位稳定性

Conclusion: 提出的方法使内存受限的AI集成移动机器人能够高效利用稠密地图，解决了内存约束下的地图管理问题，实现了稳定的定位和鲁棒的VLM操作

Abstract: Recent advances in large AI models (VLMs and LLMs) and joint use of the 3D dense maps, enable mobile robots to provide more powerful and interactive services grounded in rich spatial context. However, deploying both heavy AI models and dense maps on edge robots is challenging under strict memory budgets. When the memory budget is exceeded, required keyframes may not be loaded in time, which can degrade the stability of position estimation and interfering model performance. We proposes a semantic zone-based map management approach to stabilize dense-map utilization under memory constraints. We associate keyframes with semantic indoor regions (e.g., rooms and corridors) and keyframe management at the semantic zone level prioritizes spatially relevant map content while respecting memory constraints. This reduces keyframe loading and unloading frequency and memory usage. We evaluate the proposed approach in large-scale simulated indoor environments and on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano under concurrent SLAM-VLM execution. With Qwen3.5:0.8b, the proposed method improves throughput by 3.3 tokens/s and reduces latency by 21.7% relative to a geometric map-management strategy. Furthermore, while the geometric strategy suffers from out-of-memory failures and stalled execution under memory pressure, the proposed method eliminates both issues, preserving localization stability and enabling robust VLM operation. These results demonstrate that the proposed approach enables efficient dense map utilization for memory constrained, AI-integrated mobile robots. Code is available at: https://github.com/huichangs/rtabmap/tree/segment

</details>


### [145] [Design and Aerodynamic Modeling of MetaMorpher: A Hybrid Rotary andFixed-Wing Morphing UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29646)
*Anja Bosak,Dorian Erić,Ana Milas,Stjepan Bogdan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MetaMorpher变形无人机，通过非线性数学模型和模块化机翼设计，实现垂直起降敏捷性与固定翼巡航效率的结合


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统无人机在垂直起降敏捷性和固定翼巡航效率之间的权衡问题，需要一种能够灵活变形的无人机设计

Method: 基于spincopter平台，采用轻质材料和新型机翼折叠策略的简化机械架构，建立非线性飞行动力学模型，支持分段机翼结构的任意力分布

Result: 模型在不同结构配置下表现出可预测行为，证明了其作为快速设计评估工具的可靠性

Conclusion: MetaMorpher变形无人机模型为无人机设计提供了有效的评估工具，能够平衡垂直起降和巡航效率的需求

Abstract: In this paper, we present a generalized, comprehensive nonlinear mathematical model and conceptual design for the MetaMorpher, a metamorphic Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) designed to bridge the gap between vertical takeoff and landing agility and fixed-wing cruising efficiency. Building on the successful design of the spincopter platform, this work introduces a simplified mechanical architecture using lightweight materials and a novel wing-folding strategy. Unlike traditional rigid-body approximations, we derive a nonlinear flight dynamics model that enables arbitrary force distributions across a segmented wing structure. This modularity allows for testing different airfoils, mass distributions, and chord lengths in a single environment. As part of this work, various flight modes were specifically tested and analyzed in the Simulink environment. The results show that the model behaves predictably under different structural configurations, demonstrating its reliability as a tool for rapid design evaluation.

</details>


### [146] [SafeDMPs: Integrating Formal Safety with DMPs for Adaptive HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29708)
*Soumyodipta Nath,Pranav Tiwari,Ravi Prakash*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeDMPs框架将动态运动基元（DMPs）的闭环形式效率与时空管（STTs）的可证明安全控制律相结合，实现了无需在线优化的实时安全机器人运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人机协作环境中需要同时具备抗干扰能力和可证明的安全性。现有方法存在矛盾：DMPs具有稳定性和泛化能力但缺乏安全保证，而CBFs等正式方法提供安全保证但依赖计算昂贵的实时优化，难以用于高频控制。

Method: 提出SafeDMPs框架，将DMPs的闭环形式效率和动态鲁棒性与基于时空管（STTs）的可证明安全、非优化控制律相结合。该方法生成既能抗干扰、适应新目标，又能保证避开静态和动态障碍物的运动。

Result: 在7自由度机器人机械臂上的实验表明，SafeDMPs比基于优化的基线方法快几个数量级且更准确，实现了传统需要在线优化问题的闭环形式解。

Conclusion: SafeDMPs解决了实时安全机器人控制的效率与安全性权衡问题，为协作机器人提供了理想的实时、安全解决方案。

Abstract: Robots operating in human-centric environments must be both robust to disturbances and provably safe from collisions. Achieving these properties simultaneously and efficiently remains a central challenge. While Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) offer inherent stability and generalization from single demonstrations, they lack formal safety guarantees. Conversely, formal methods like Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) provide provable safety but often rely on computationally expensive, real-time optimization, hindering their use in high-frequency control. This paper introduces SafeDMPs, a novel framework that resolves this trade-off. We integrate the closed-form efficiency and dynamic robustness of DMPs with a provably safe, non-optimization-based control law derived from Spatio-Temporal Tubes (STTs). This synergy allows us to generate motions that are not only robust to perturbations and adaptable to new goals, but also guaranteed to avoid static and dynamic obstacles. Our approach achieves a closed-form solution for a problem that traditionally requires online optimization. Experimental results on a 7-DOF robot manipulator demonstrate that SafeDMPs is orders of magnitude faster and more accurate than optimization-based baselines, making it an ideal solution for real-time, safe, and collaborative robotics.

</details>


### [147] [Reconfiguration of supernumerary robotic limbs for human augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29808)
*Mustafa Mete,Anastasia Bolotnikova,Alexander Schuessler,Jamie Paik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于定量人体增强分析的可重构超数机器人肢体框架，通过模块化设计提升在非结构化环境中的适应性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超数机器人肢体主要针对结构化工业环境，缺乏对动态非结构化环境的适应性，需要更灵活的增强方案

Method: 建立可重构SRL框架，定义人体增强比率评估协作、可见扩展和非可见扩展工作空间，指导SRL配置选择和自主性设计

Result: 通过折纸启发的模块化可重构SRL实验验证，定量增强分析能有效指导SRL重构和控制以匹配任务需求

Conclusion: 基于定量人体增强分析的可重构SRL为日常环境中的适应性人体增强和辅助提供了新视角

Abstract: Wearable robots aim to seamlessly adapt to humans and their environment with personalized interactions. Existing supernumerary robotic limbs (SRLs), which enhance the physical capabilities of humans with additional extremities, have thus far been developed primarily for task-specific applications in structured industrial settings, limiting their adaptability to dynamic and unstructured environments. Here, we introduce a novel reconfigurable SRL framework grounded in a quantitative analysis of human augmentation to guide the development of more adaptable SRLs for diverse scenarios. This framework captures how SRL configuration shapes workspace extension and human-robot collaboration. We define human augmentation ratios to evaluate collaborative, visible extended, and non-visible extended workspaces, enabling systematic selection of SRL placement, morphology, and autonomy for a given task. Using these metrics, we demonstrate how quantitative augmentation analysis can guide the reconfiguration and control of SRLs to better match task requirements. We validate the proposed approach through experiments with a reconfigurable SRL composed of origami-inspired modular elements. Our results suggest that reconfigurable SRLs, informed by quantitative human augmentation analysis, offer a new perspective for providing adaptable human augmentation and assistance in everyday environments.

</details>


### [148] [DIAL: Decoupling Intent and Action via Latent World Modeling for End-to-End VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29844)
*Yi Chen,Yuying Ge,Hui Zhou,Mingyu Ding,Yixiao Ge,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DIAL框架通过可微分潜在意图瓶颈连接高级决策与低级运动执行，利用VLM进行潜在世界建模，实现更稳定高效的机器人操作学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端VLA模型主要将VLM作为多模态编码器，直接将视觉语言特征映射到低级动作，这未能充分利用VLM在高级决策中的潜力，并导致训练不稳定和语义表示退化。

Method: 提出DIAL框架：1) VLM-based System-2在VLM原生特征空间中进行潜在世界建模，合成潜在视觉前瞻作为意图瓶颈；2) 轻量级System-1策略将预测意图与当前观测解码为精确机器人动作；3) 采用两阶段训练：解耦预热阶段学习预测潜在未来和运动控制，然后进行端到端联合优化。

Result: 在RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop基准测试中达到新的SOTA，仅需先前方法1/10的演示数据；通过利用异构人类演示学习物理基础操作先验，在真实人形机器人部署中展现对未见物体和新配置的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: DIAL框架通过可微分潜在意图瓶颈有效连接高级决策与低级执行，在保持预训练知识的同时实现稳定优化，显著提升机器人操作性能并减少数据需求。

Abstract: The development of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models has been significantly accelerated by pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs). However, most existing end-to-end VLAs treat the VLM primarily as a multimodal encoder, directly mapping vision-language features to low-level actions. This paradigm underutilizes the VLM's potential in high-level decision making and introduces training instability, frequently degrading its rich semantic representations. To address these limitations, we introduce DIAL, a framework bridging high-level decision making and low-level motor execution through a differentiable latent intent bottleneck. Specifically, a VLM-based System-2 performs latent world modeling by synthesizing latent visual foresight within the VLM's native feature space; this foresight explicitly encodes intent and serves as the structural bottleneck. A lightweight System-1 policy then decodes this predicted intent together with the current observation into precise robot actions via latent inverse dynamics. To ensure optimization stability, we employ a two-stage training paradigm: a decoupled warmup phase where System-2 learns to predict latent futures while System-1 learns motor control under ground-truth future guidance within a unified feature space, followed by seamless end-to-end joint optimization. This enables action-aware gradients to refine the VLM backbone in a controlled manner, preserving pre-trained knowledge. Extensive experiments on the RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop benchmark show that DIAL establishes a new state-of-the-art, achieving superior performance with 10x fewer demonstrations than prior methods. Furthermore, by leveraging heterogeneous human demonstrations, DIAL learns physically grounded manipulation priors and exhibits robust zero-shot generalization to unseen objects and novel configurations during real-world deployment on a humanoid robot.

</details>


### [149] [Passive iFIR filters for data-driven velocity control in robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29882)
*Yi Zhang,Zixing Wang,Fulvio Forni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于被动性约束的数据驱动速度控制方法，使用3分钟探测数据学习iFIR控制器，在Franka机器人上比优化PID提升74.5%跟踪性能，且保持闭环稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习控制方法缺乏稳定性保证，而传统控制方法（如PID）性能有限。需要一种既能从数据中学习又能保证稳定性的控制方法。

Method: 使用VRFT（虚拟参考反馈整定）方法，仅需3分钟探测数据，设计被动iFIR（无限脉冲响应）控制器。通过被动性约束保证闭环稳定性，控制器可在机器人动力学变化时重新学习。

Result: 在Franka Research 3机器人上，关节空间和笛卡尔空间速度控制均优于VRFT整定的PID基线，笛卡尔速度跟踪误差最多减少74.5%。控制器在动力学变化后可重新学习恢复性能。

Conclusion: 该方法连接了学习控制与稳定性保证设计，被动iFIR既能从数据学习又保持被动性稳定性保证，为非线性机器人控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a passive, data-driven velocity control method for nonlinear robotic manipulators that achieves better tracking performance than optimized PID with comparable design complexity. Using only three minutes of probing data, a VRFT-based design identifies passive iFIR controllers that (i) preserve closed-loop stability via passivity constraints and (ii) outperform a VRFT-tuned PID baseline on the Franka Research 3 robot in both joint-space and Cartesian-space velocity control, achieving up to a 74.5% reduction in tracking error for the Cartesian velocity tracking experiment with the most demanding reference model. When the robot end-effector dynamics change, the controller can be re-learned from new data, regaining nominal performance. This study bridges learning-based control and stability-guaranteed design: passive iFIR learns from data while retaining passivity-based stability guarantees, unlike many learning-based approaches.

</details>


### [150] [Hybrid Framework for Robotic Manipulation: Integrating Reinforcement Learning and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30022)
*Md Saad,Sajjad Hussain,Mohd Suhaib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合强化学习与大型语言模型的混合框架，用于提升机器人操作任务性能，在仿真环境中测试显示任务完成时间减少33.5%，准确性和适应性分别提升18.1%和36.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统在处理复杂、类人指令和适应动态环境方面存在局限，需要将低层控制与高层推理有效结合，以提升机器人系统的效率、适应性和人机交互能力。

Method: 提出混合框架：使用强化学习负责精确的低层控制，大型语言模型负责高层任务规划和自然语言理解，通过PyBullet仿真环境中的Franka Emika Panda机械臂进行测试。

Result: 相比纯强化学习系统，任务完成时间减少33.5%，准确性提升18.1%，适应性提升36.4%，证明了LLM增强型机器人系统在实际应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: 该混合框架有效连接了机器人系统的低层执行与高层推理，使机器人能够理解和执行复杂指令并实时适应环境变化，未来研究将探索仿真到现实的迁移、可扩展性和多机器人系统。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new hybrid framework that combines Reinforcement Learning (RL) and Large Language Models (LLMs) to improve robotic manipulation tasks. By utilizing RL for accurate low-level control and LLMs for high level task planning and understanding of natural language, the proposed framework effectively connects low-level execution with high-level reasoning in robotic systems. This integration allows robots to understand and carry out complex, human-like instructions while adapting to changing environments in real time. The framework is tested in a PyBullet-based simulation environment using the Franka Emika Panda robotic arm, with various manipulation scenarios as benchmarks. The results show a 33.5% decrease in task completion time and enhancements of 18.1% and 36.4% in accuracy and adaptability, respectively, when compared to systems that use only RL. These results underscore the potential of LLM-enhanced robotic systems for practical applications, making them more efficient, adaptable, and capable of interacting with humans. Future research will aim to explore sim-to-real transfer, scalability, and multi-robot systems to further broaden the framework's applicability.

</details>


### [151] [HapCompass: A Rotational Haptic Device for Contact-Rich Robotic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30042)
*Xiangshan Tan,Jingtian Ji,Tianchong Jiang,Pedro Lopes,Matthew R. Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HapCompass：一种低成本可穿戴触觉设备，通过机械旋转单个线性谐振执行器提供2D方向提示，提升遥操作接触丰富任务的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务对机器人遥操作构成重大挑战。虽然触觉反馈对接触丰富任务至关重要，但在可穿戴遥操作界面中提供直观的方向提示仍然是一个瓶颈。现有解决方案（如手持控制器的非定向振动）提供的信息有限，而振动触觉阵列容易产生感知干扰。

Method: 提出HapCompass，一种新颖的低成本可穿戴触觉设备，通过机械旋转单个线性谐振执行器（LRA）来渲染2D方向提示。评估了该设备向人类操作者传递方向提示的能力，并与仅视觉和非定向反馈基线进行比较。

Result: HapCompass提高了遥操作任务的成功率，减少了完成时间和最大接触力。初步的模仿学习评估表明，HapCompass提供的定向反馈提高了演示数据的质量，进而提升了训练策略的性能。

Conclusion: HapCompass是一种有效的低成本触觉设备，能够提供直观的2D方向提示，显著改善接触丰富遥操作任务的性能，并为模仿学习提供更高质量的演示数据。

Abstract: The contact-rich nature of manipulation makes it a significant challenge for robotic teleoperation. While haptic feedback is critical for contact-rich tasks, providing intuitive directional cues within wearable teleoperation interfaces remains a bottleneck. Existing solutions, such as non-directional vibrations from handheld controllers, provide limited information, while vibrotactile arrays are prone to perceptual interference. To address these limitations, we propose HapCompass, a novel, low-cost wearable haptic device that renders 2D directional cues by mechanically rotating a single linear resonant actuator (LRA). We evaluated HapCompass's ability to convey directional cues to human operators and showed that it increased the success rate, decreased the completion time and the maximum contact force for teleoperated manipulation tasks when compared to vision-only and non-directional feedback baselines. Furthermore, we conducted a preliminary imitation-learning evaluation, suggesting that the directional feedback provided by HapCompass enhances the quality of demonstration data and, in turn, the trained policy. We release the design of the HapCompass device along with the code that implements our teleoperation interface: https://ripl.github.io/HapCompass/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [ChartDiff: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Comprehending Pairs of Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28902)
*Rongtian Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChartDiff是首个大规模跨图表比较总结基准，包含8,541个图表对，用于评估模型在跨图表比较推理方面的能力，发现当前视觉语言模型在此任务上仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表理解基准主要关注单图表解释，缺乏对多图表比较推理的评估。为了填补这一空白，需要创建专门针对跨图表比较总结的基准数据集。

Method: 构建ChartDiff基准，包含8,541个图表对，涵盖多样数据源、图表类型和视觉风格。使用LLM生成并人工验证的摘要来描述趋势、波动和异常差异。评估通用模型、图表专用模型和基于流水线的方法。

Result: 前沿通用模型在GPT评估中获得最高质量，而专用和流水线方法获得更高的ROUGE分数但人类对齐评估较低，显示词汇重叠与实际摘要质量不匹配。多序列图表对所有模型家族都具有挑战性，而强大的端到端模型对绘图库差异相对稳健。

Conclusion: 比较性图表推理对当前视觉语言模型仍是一个重大挑战，ChartDiff可作为推进多图表理解研究的新基准。

Abstract: Charts are central to analytical reasoning, yet existing benchmarks for chart understanding focus almost exclusively on single-chart interpretation rather than comparative reasoning across multiple charts. To address this gap, we introduce ChartDiff, the first large-scale benchmark for cross-chart comparative summarization. ChartDiff consists of 8,541 chart pairs spanning diverse data sources, chart types, and visual styles, each annotated with LLM-generated and human-verified summaries describing differences in trends, fluctuations, and anomalies. Using ChartDiff, we evaluate general-purpose, chart-specialized, and pipeline-based models.
